## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner for work that needs a place or takes a while. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 직접 작업을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 저장소에서 깨진 테스트 고쳐줘"처럼 말씀해 주시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] Korean reply needed. The user says thanks and goodbye until tomorrow morning. Simple closing.
  [reminder set] 내일 아침에 다시 이야기하기로 해서 알려드려요
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 오늘 하루도 수고 많았어요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봬요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届いたことを確かめながら、順番どおり確実に届ける」通信方式で、UDPは「確認せず、速さを優先して送りっぱなしにする」通信方式です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla, si quieres un historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar tal cual cuándo se integró el trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、可靠有序；UDP 无连接、不保证送达和顺序，但更快、开销更小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local, unpushed commits onto the latest base to keep history clean before you share them. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have pulled.
```

  [partner ack line] 好，我按顺序做：先换新输入框，再接短信验证码，最后补端到端测试，每件做完告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页重做 1/3：换新输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。" ⏎  ⏎ 本任务只做第一件：把登录页的登录表单换成新的输入框组件。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准： ⏎ - 登录表单的输入框全部改用新的输入框组件，旧的输入框用法不再留在登录表单里。 ⏎ - 表单行为不变（输入、提交、校验提示照旧）。 ⏎ - 现有测试和类型检查全部通过，报告里附上实际运行的命令和输出结果。 ⏎  ⏎ 不做的事： ⏎ - 不做短信验证码（下一件）。 ⏎ - 不补端到端测试（第三件）。 ⏎ - 不推送、不合并到主分支、不开 PR。 ⏎ - 修改在独立分支上提交即可。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：仓库 ~/dev/ducter。
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页重做 2/3：接上短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页重做 3/3：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做

```bash

```

也可以只给文件名的一部分，Bun 会按路径匹配：

```bash
bun test lru
```

如果只想跑某个测试用例（按名字匹配），加 `-t`：

```bash
bun test -t "过期"
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着 compaction 那个草稿 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细，改好了告诉你。
  [reminder set] PR #4127 锁粒度改好并且测试通过
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话：「接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点」

背景：这是同一件事的延续。草稿 PR #4127（分支 ducter/compaction-scheduler）还没合并，现在要按 review 意见把锁粒度改细。

要做的：
1. 先读 PR #4127 上的 review 评论，找出关于锁粒度的意见（是哪把锁、哪段临界区太大）。
2. 只改锁的粒度：缩小持锁范围，让 compaction 调度不再整段持有大锁，其它读写路径能并行。不改对外行为，不顺手重构别的。
3. 跑相关测试，确认 compaction 的正确性没变（测试输出作为证据报告）。
4. 改动提交在当前分支上。

完成标准：review 里提到的锁粒度问题都处理了，测试通过，报告里说明每处改了什么、为什么这样改。

不做的事：不 push 到远端，不回复或关闭 PR 上的
  [also used tools] task_read, wait_for, wait_cancel
```

    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">2 班</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">车次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <script>
    function render(fares) {
      fares = fares || [];
      var under = fares.filter(function (f) { return f.price < 600; });
      document.getElementById('count').textContent = under.length + ' 班';
      var min = under.reduce(function (m, f) { return m === null || f.price < m ? f.price : m; }, null);
      document.getElementById('sub').textContent = min === null ? '暂无 600 以下的票' : '最低 ¥' + min + ' · 每天早上更新';
      var list = document.getElementById('list');
      list.innerHTML = '';
      fares.forEach(function (f) {
        var row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'k-row';
        row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + f.train + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-sub">' + (f.seats == null ? '余票未知' : '二等座余 ' + f.seats + ' 张') + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-value" style="color:' + (f.price < 600 ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)') + '">¥' + f.price + '</div>';
        list.appendChild(row);
      });
      if (!fares.length) list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">还没有票价数据</div>';
    }
    ducter.onChange(function () { render(ducter.get('fares')); });
    render(ducter.get('fares'));
  </script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"fares":[{"train":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"train":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"train":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}

<script>
function render(){
  var runs = ducter.get('runs') || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function(r){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div'); main.className = 'k-row-main'; main.textContent = 'Night ' + r.night;
    var sub = document.createElement('div'); sub.className = 'k-row-sub'; sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms · compaction stalls ' + r.stalls;
    var val = document.createElement('div'); val.className = 'k-row-value'; val.textContent = r.writes + ' writes/s';
    row.appendChild(main); row.appendChild(sub); row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
  if (last) {
    document.getElementById('sv').textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
    document.getElementById('ss').textContent = 'Night ' + last.night + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms';
  }
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>

<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+n.toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const sum=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?fmt(sum):"¥0";
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  if(!xs.length){const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="还没有花销";list.appendChild(e);return;}
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(Number(x.yuan)||0);
    const wrap=document.createElement("div");wrap.appendChild(main);wrap.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(wrap);row.appendChild(val);list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(sum));
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

    const val=document.createElement('div'); val.className='k-row-value'; val.textContent=money(e.amt);
    const body=document.createElement('div'); body.append(main,meta);
    row.append(body,val);
    row.onclick=()=>{
      const open=row.querySelector('.k-del');
      if(open){open.remove(); return;}
      const del=document.createElement('button');
      del.className='k-btn k-del'; del.textContent='Remove';
      del.onclick=ev=>{ev.stopPropagation(); ducter.set('expenses', items.filter(x=>x.id!==e.id));};
      row.appendChild(del);
    };
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  ducter.summary(items.length? money(tot)+' this month' : 'Nothing logged yet');
}

$('save').onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if(!(amt>0)) { $('amt').focus(); return; }
  const entry={id:uid(), amt:Math.round(amt*100)/100, cat, note:$('note').value.trim(), date:today()};
  ducter.set('expenses', [entry, ...items]);
  $('amt').value=''; $('note').value='';
};

drawCats();
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('expenses')).then(v=>{items=Array.isArray(v)?v:[]; render();});
ducter.onChange(()=>Promise.resolve(ducter.get('expenses')).then(v=>{items=Array.isArray(v)?v:[]; render();}));
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

(function(){

  var pad = function(n){ return String(n).padStart(2,'0'); };
  function todayStr(){ var d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear()+'-'+pad(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+pad(d.getDate()); }
  function nowHM(){ var d = new Date(); return  … l ? total + ' ml · ' + list.length + ' 次' : '今天还没记');
  }

  $('list').onclick = function(e){
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    var i = +row.getAttribute('data-i'), arr = feeds(), f = arr[i];
    if (!f) return;
    ducter.ask('删掉 ' + f.t + ' 的 ' + f.ml + ' ml？').then(function(a){
      if (!a) return;
      var cur = feeds();
      cur.splice(i, 1);

  [60, 90, 120, 150].forEach(function(v){

    b.onclick = function(){ $('ml').value = v; };
    $('chips').appendChild(b);
  });

  $('add').onclick = function(){
    var t = $('tm').value.trim() || nowHM();
    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t)) { $('tm').value = ''; $('tm').placeholder = '格式如 14:30'; return; }
    var ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
    if (!ml || ml <= 0) return;
    var arr = feeds();
    arr.push({ d: todayStr(), t: t, ml: ml });
    ducter.set('feeds', arr);
    $('tm').value = ''; $('ml').value = '';
    render();
  };

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();

  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub">PRs waiting on someone. Tap one when it lands.</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row { width:100%; background:none; border:0; text-align:left; cursor:pointer; color:var(--d-ink); }
  .k-row.landed .k-row-main { text-decoration:line-through; color:var(--d-muted); }
  .k-tick { display:inline-block; width:18px; height:18px; border:1.5px solid var(--d-line); border-radius:4px; margin-right:8px; vertical-align:middle; text-align:center; line-height:15px; font-size:12px; }
  .k-row.landed .k-tick { background:var(--d-good); border-color:var(--d-good); color:var(--d-bg); }
</style>
<script>
  var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
  var landed = {};

  function esc(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]; }); }

  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var html = '';
    var open = 0;
    PRS.forEach(function (p) {
      var done = !!landed[p.id];
      if (!done) open++;
      html += '<button class="k-row' + (done ? ' landed' : '') + '" data-id="' + p.id + '">' +
        '<span class="k-row-main"><span class="k-tick">' + (done ? '✓' : '') + '</span>' + esc(p.title) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="k-row-sub">' + esc(p.repo) + ' · ' + (done ? 'Landed' : esc(p.wait)) + '</span>' +
        '</button>';
    });
    list.innerHTML = html;
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = open;
    ducter.summary(open + ' still waiting');
  }

  document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    var id = row.getAttribute('data-id');
    landed[id] = !landed[id];
    ducter.set('landed', landed);
    render();
  });

  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function (v) {
      landed = v || {};
      render();
    });
  });

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function (v) {
    landed = v || {};
    render();
  });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","wait":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"mem10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","wait":"CI failing"},{"id":"hawdb1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","wait":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","wait":"Draft, not sent for review yet"}]}

适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕正好表达"这里还没有内容"，而且有种"可以开始写点什么"的暗示。颜色柔和，不会让人觉得出错了，也和大多数界面的配色搭得上。

有两点要注意：这张图只有 320×246，放大或在高分屏上会发虚，最好找一张 2 倍的版本。另外它是单独一个物件，配一行提示文字和一个按钮会更清楚，比如"还没有记录，点这里新建一条"。
